<tr><td>prototyping</td><td>prōtōtȳping</td><td>pro-to-typ-ing</td><td>prō-tō-tȳp-ing</td><td>n. 原型设计, 样机制作</td></tr>

<tr><td>prototypically</td><td>prōtōtẏpicållẏ</td><td>pro-to-typ-ic-al-ly</td><td>prō-tō-tẏp-ic-ål-lẏ</td><td>adv. 典型地</td></tr>
